📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • MPXV 预先暴露于精液会显着增加原代人成纤维细胞和角质形成细胞的感染。SEVI 增强了上皮细胞、原代单核细胞和离体人体皮肤以及临床 MPXV 分离株和其他 正痘病毒 的感染。SEVI 增加了病毒粒子的附着和摄取,而 CLR01 破坏了精液淀粉样蛋白并消除了增强。
  • 参与者描述了对 猴痘 的有限且不断发展的知识,信息主要通过社交媒体、同行网络和新闻媒体获得;猴痘 消息经常被认为是侮辱性的和基于身份的,塑造了易感性的看法并加剧了对歧视的担忧。 [10.1177/10872914261471238]。
  • 结构叠加显示人源化变体和亲本单克隆抗体之间具有高度构象保守性(RMSD < 0.6 Å),两种变体都保留了具有天然人性特征的完整表位特异性,并且与 7D11 相比,POX1.1 对痘苗病毒 (VACV) 和 MPXV 的中和作用增强。
  • 该混合模型在 Monkeypox 数据集上实现了 95.17% 的准确度和 95.04% F1 分数,在 COVID-19 数据集上实现了 92.15% 的准确度和 91.87% F1 分数,在 SemEval-2014 上实现了 87.68% 的准确度和 87.12% F1 分数,优于传统和基于 Transformer 的基线。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Pre-exposure of MPXV to seminal fluid markedly increased infection of primary human fibroblasts and keratinocytes.
  • Participants described limited and evolving knowledge of Mpox, with information obtained primarily through social media, peer networks, and news media; Mpox messaging was frequently perceived as stigmatizing and identity-based, shaping perceptions of susceptibility and contributing to concerns about discrimination.
  • Structural superimposition showed high conformational conservation between humanised variants and parental mAbs (RMSD < 0.6 Å), both variants retained full epitope specificity with natural humanness profiles, and POX1.1 exhibited enhanced neutralisation against vaccinia virus (VACV) and MPXV compared with 7D11.
  • The hybrid model achieved 95.17% accuracy and 95.04% F1-score on the Monkeypox dataset, 92.15% accuracy and 91.87% F1-score on the COVID-19 dataset, and 87.68% accuracy and 87.12% F1-score on SemEval-2014, outperforming traditional and transformer-based baselines.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMC Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-21  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12916-026-05068-4  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

从行为相关性到结构性相关性:对全球报告的 猴痘 的多阶段分析

From behavioral to structural correlates: a multi-phase analysis of global reported mpox
作者： Li Shen, Mengna Wei, Yuetong Chen, Rui Li, Zhenfan Xu, Yaqin Su, Mengyan Ye, Zhenhua Lu, Jiayi Li, Jinzhan Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 猴痘 流行病学 已从地方性人畜共患 跨物种溢出 扩展到持续的人传人 传播。虽然行为风险因素已得到充分认识,但 猴痘 负担在不同流行阶段之间的宏观关联如何变化仍没有得到充分量化,从而限制了有针对性的公共卫生应对措施。方法利用世界卫生组织(WHO)2022年1月1日至2025年4月30日的全球每日猴痘 监测数据,计算国家时期报告的猴痘 发病率,并开发了一个整合不同流行阶段的多领域宏观层面变量的阶段敏感分析框架。对五个集成机器学习模型(极限梯度提升、梯度提升决策树、轻梯度提升机、随机森林、分类提升)进行了优化,并使用 SHapley 加法解释 (SHAP) 值解释它们的输出,以识别跨阶段 猴痘 负担变化的相关性。报告结果 猴痘 发病率 表现出四个时间流行病学阶段,包括局部零星出现、全球加速传播、持续传播和区域特定复发。基于 SHAP 的解释揭示了模型相关相关性的阶段依赖性转变,从早期阶段的行为因素(例如密切接触网络和人口流动性)到后来与社会经济差异和卫生系统能力相关的结构脆弱性指标。LGBT+(女同性恋、男同性恋、双性恋、跨性别者和其他性少数群体)法律指数是最强的总体预测因子,占 SHAP 总体重要性的 13.5%。它与报告的 猴痘 发病率 的关联取决于具体情况,在法律包容性环境中出现积极贡献,在限制性环境中观察到消极贡献,这种模式与差异案例确定和报告可见性一致。结论 我们的研究结果强调了全球 猴痘 负担从行为指标到结构指标的转变,揭示了特定阶段和地理异质性。这些结果支持阶段性干预措施,将早期有针对性的预防与后期卫生系统强化、公平的 监测、社区支持和减少耻辱相结合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 流行病学 已从地方性人畜共患 跨物种溢出 扩展到持续的人传人 传播。虽然行为风险因素已得到充分认识,但 猴痘 负担在不同流行阶段之间的宏观关联如何变化仍没有得到充分量化,从而限制了有针对性的公共卫生应对措施。
设计与对象
我们利用世界卫生组织(WHO)2022年1月1日至2025年4月30日的全球每日猴痘 监测数据,计算了国家时期报告的猴痘 发病率,并开发了一个整合不同流行阶段的多领域宏观层面变量的阶段敏感分析框架。
核心方法
对五个集成机器学习模型(极限梯度提升、梯度提升决策树、轻梯度提升机、随机森林、分类提升)进行了优化,并使用 SHapley 加法解释 (SHAP) 值解释它们的输出,以识别跨阶段 猴痘 负担变化的相关性。
主要结果
报告的猴痘 发病率表现出四个时间流行病学阶段,包括局部零星出现、加速全球传播、持续传播和特定区域复苏。基于 SHAP 的解释揭示了模型相关相关性的阶段依赖性转变,从早期阶段的行为因素(例如密切接触网络和人口流动性)到后来与社会经济差异和卫生系统能力相关的结构脆弱性指标。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果强调了全球 猴痘 负担从行为指标到结构指标的转变,揭示了特定阶段和地理异质性。LGBT+ 法律指数是最强的整体预测因子,占 SHAP 整体重要性的 13.5%,其上下文相关的关联反映了差异案例确定和报告可见性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持阶段性干预措施,将早期有针对性的预防与后期卫生系统强化、公平的 监测、社区支持和减少耻辱相结合。研究结果为优化不同流行阶段的公共卫生应对措施提供了可行的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于报告的 监测 数据,这些数据可能会受到不同环境下不同确定和报告偏差的影响。尽管存在这些限制,大规模、多相分析和强大的集成建模方法为已识别的特定相相关性提供了适度的信心。

From behavioral to structural correlates: a multi-phase analysis of global reported mpox

Authors: Li Shen, Mengna Wei, Yuetong Chen, Rui Li, Zhenfan Xu, Yaqin Su, Mengyan Ye, Zhenhua Lu, Jiayi Li, Jinzhan Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Mpox epidemiology has expanded from endemic zoonotic spillover to sustained human-to-human transmission. While behavioral risk factors have been well recognized, how macro-level correlates of mpox burden vary across epidemic phases remains insufficiently quantified, limiting targeted public health responses. Methods Using World Health Organization (WHO) global daily mpox surveillance data from January 1, 2022 to April 30, 2025, we calculated country-period reported mpox incidence and developed a phase-sensitive analytical framework integrating multi-domain macro-level variables across distinct epidemic phases. Five ensemble machine learning models (eXtreme Gradient Boosting, Gradient Boosting Decision Tree, Light Gradient Boosting Machine, Random Forest, Categorical Boosting) were optimized, and their outputs were interpreted using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) values to identify the changing correlates of mpox burden across phases. Results Reported mpox incidence exhibited four temporal-epidemiological phases, comprising localized sporadic emergence, accelerated global spread, sustained dissemination, and region-specific resurgence. SHAP-based interpretation revealed a phase-dependent shift in model-associated correlates, from behavioral factors in earlier phases, such as close-contact networks and population mobility, to later structural vulnerability indicators related to socioeconomic disparities and health-system capacity. The LGBT+ (lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, and other sexual and gender minorities) Legal Index was the strongest overall predictor, accounting for 13.5% of the overall SHAP importance. Its association with reported mpox incidence was context-dependent, with positive contributions occurring in legally inclusive settings and negative contributions observed in restrictive settings, a pattern consistent with differential case ascertainment and reporting visibility. Conclusions Our findings highlight a transition from behavioral to structural indicators in global mpox burden, uncovering phase-specific and geographic heterogeneity. These results support phase-tailored interventions that combine early targeted prevention with later health-system strengthening, equitable surveillance, community support, and stigma reduction.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox epidemiology has expanded from endemic zoonotic spillover to sustained human-to-human transmission. While behavioral risk factors have been well recognized, how macro-level correlates of mpox burden vary across epidemic phases remains insufficiently quantified, limiting targeted public health responses.
Design and population
Using World Health Organization (WHO) global daily mpox surveillance data from January 1, 2022 to April 30, 2025, we calculated country-period reported mpox incidence and developed a phase-sensitive analytical framework integrating multi-domain macro-level variables across distinct epidemic phases.
Core methods
Five ensemble machine learning models (eXtreme Gradient Boosting, Gradient Boosting Decision Tree, Light Gradient Boosting Machine, Random Forest, Categorical Boosting) were optimized, and their outputs were interpreted using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) values to identify the changing correlates of mpox burden across phases.
Main results
Reported mpox incidence exhibited four temporal-epidemiological phases, comprising localized sporadic emergence, accelerated global spread, sustained dissemination, and region-specific resurgence. SHAP-based interpretation revealed a phase-dependent shift in model-associated correlates, from behavioral factors in earlier phases, such as close-contact networks and population mobility, to later structural vulnerability indicators related to socioeconomic disparities and health-system capacity.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings highlight a transition from behavioral to structural indicators in global mpox burden, uncovering phase-specific and geographic heterogeneity. The LGBT+ Legal Index was the strongest overall predictor, accounting for 13.5% of the overall SHAP importance, with context-dependent associations reflecting differential case ascertainment and reporting visibility.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support phase-tailored interventions that combine early targeted prevention with later health-system strengthening, equitable surveillance, community support, and stigma reduction. The findings provide actionable insights for optimizing public health responses across different epidemic phases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on reported surveillance data, which may be subject to varying ascertainment and reporting biases across settings. Despite these limitations, the large-scale, multi-phase analysis and robust ensemble modeling approach provide moderate confidence in the identified phase-specific correlates.

患有严重 猴痘 感染的免疫功能低下个体的 进化 对 特考韦瑞 耐药性。

The evolution of tecovirimat resistance in an immunocompromised individual with severe mpox infection.
作者： Salmaan Bholah, Chris Davis, Katherine Smollett, Richard J Orton, Temi Lampejo, Diogo Correa Mendonca, Nicole S. Upfold, Amy Nettlemill, Matthew Hewitt, Hassan Hartman
摘要中文翻译特考韦瑞 广泛用于治疗严重的 猴痘,但耐药性的报道越来越多,特别是在免疫功能低下的 宿主 中。我们描述了一名患有晚期 HIV(CD4 23 个细胞/μL)和播散性坏死性疾病的 43 岁男性的持续进化枝 IIb 猴痘 病例。尽管特考韦瑞治疗时间较长,但病变仍在进展。45 天内获得的系列病毒分离株 (n=8) 进行了表型敏感性测试和全 基因组 测序。特考韦瑞 敏感性显着下降,晚期分离株的 IC50 增加约 3 log10。测序鉴定出不同的耐药相关 VP37 突变(A290V、A295E),它们在异质病毒群体中同时出现。体外传代证实了药物压力下耐药表型的快速富集。对西多福韦和布西多福韦的敏感性得以保留,并且开始使用西多福韦与临床改善和新病变的停止相关。该病例证明了严重 猴痘 中多谱系 特考韦瑞 的快速内部 宿主 进化 耐药性,强调了免疫功能低下患者中对 监测 耐药性和替代或组合 抗病毒药物 策略的需求。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
特考韦瑞 广泛用于治疗严重的 猴痘,但耐药性的报道越来越多,特别是在免疫功能低下的 宿主 中。
设计与对象
我们描述了一名患有晚期 HIV(CD4 23 个细胞/μL)和播散性坏死性疾病的 43 岁男性的持续进化枝 IIb 猴痘 病例。
核心方法
45 天内获得的系列病毒分离株 (n=8) 进行了表型敏感性测试和全 基因组 测序。
主要结果
特考韦瑞 敏感性显着下降,晚期分离株的 IC50 增加约 3 log10。对西多福韦和布西多福韦的敏感性得以保留,并且开始使用西多福韦与临床改善和新病变的停止相关。
解释与创新
该病例证明了严重 猴痘 中多谱系 特考韦瑞 的快速内部 宿主 进化 耐药性,强调了免疫功能低下患者中对 监测 耐药性和替代或组合 抗病毒药物 策略的需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了监测免疫功能低下患者中 特考韦瑞 耐药性的重要性,并支持在怀疑耐药性时使用西多福韦等替代 特考韦瑞。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于单个案例研究,限制了普遍性,但详细的表型和基因组分析为耐药性的发展提供了强有力的机制见解。

The evolution of tecovirimat resistance in an immunocompromised individual with severe mpox infection.

Authors: Salmaan Bholah, Chris Davis, Katherine Smollett, Richard J Orton, Temi Lampejo, Diogo Correa Mendonca, Nicole S. Upfold, Amy Nettlemill, Matthew Hewitt, Hassan Hartman
Original abstract (English)
Tecovirimat is widely used for severe mpox, but resistance is increasingly reported, particularly in immunocompromised hosts. We describe a case of persistent clade IIb mpox in a 43-year-old man with advanced HIV (CD4 23 cells/μL) and disseminated necrotising disease. Despite prolonged tecovirimat therapy, lesions progressed. Serial viral isolates (n=8) obtained over 45 days underwent phenotypic susceptibility testing and whole-genome sequencing. Tecovirimat susceptibility declined markedly, with a ∼3 log10 increase in IC50 in late isolates. Sequencing identified distinct resistance-associated VP37 mutations (A290V, A295E), emerging in parallel within heterogeneous viral populations. In vitro passaging confirmed rapid enrichment of resistant phenotypes under drug pressure. Susceptibility to cidofovir and brincidofovir was preserved, and initiation of cidofovir was associated with clinical improvement and cessation of new lesions. This case demonstrates rapid intra-host evolution of multi-lineage tecovirimat resistance in severe mpox, highlighting the need for resistance surveillance and alternative or combination antiviral strategies in immunocompromised patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Tecovirimat is widely used for severe mpox, but resistance is increasingly reported, particularly in immunocompromised hosts.
Design and population
We describe a case of persistent clade IIb mpox in a 43-year-old man with advanced HIV (CD4 23 cells/μL) and disseminated necrotising disease.
Core methods
Serial viral isolates (n=8) obtained over 45 days underwent phenotypic susceptibility testing and whole-genome sequencing.
Main results
Tecovirimat susceptibility declined markedly, with a ∼3 log10 increase in IC50 in late isolates. Susceptibility to cidofovir and brincidofovir was preserved, and initiation of cidofovir was associated with clinical improvement and cessation of new lesions.
Interpretation and novelty
This case demonstrates rapid intra-host evolution of multi-lineage tecovirimat resistance in severe mpox, highlighting the need for resistance surveillance and alternative or combination antiviral strategies in immunocompromised patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of monitoring tecovirimat resistance in immunocompromised patients and support the use of alternative antivirals like cidofovir when resistance is suspected.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case study, limiting generalizability, but the detailed phenotypic and genomic analyses provide strong mechanistic insight into resistance development.

精液通过淀粉样原纤维增强猴痘病毒感染

Seminal fluid enhances monkeypox virus infection via amyloidogenic fibrils
作者： Emma Torbica, Matthias Denkewitz, Tamara Quick, Nadja Zöller, Katja Steinhorst, Stefan Kippenberger, Florian Rothweiler, Jindrich Cinatl, Pia Veratti, Florian Full
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是由猴痘病毒(MPXV)引起的重新出现的 人兽共患病,最近的 暴发 是由性 传播 驱动的。然而,精液如何导致 MPXV 感染仍不清楚。在这里,我们发现人类精液可有效增强易感靶细胞中的 MPXV 感染。MPXV 预先暴露于多个供体的精液中,会显着增加原代人成纤维细胞和角质形成细胞的感染。组装成阳离子淀粉样原纤维(SEVI 和 SEM)的精液衍生肽片段增强了上皮细胞、原代单核细胞和离体人体皮肤以及临床 MPXV 分离株和其他 正痘病毒 的感染。SEVI 增加了病毒粒子的附着和摄取,而原纤维靶向分子镊子 (CLR01) 破坏了精液淀粉样蛋白并消除了增强作用。值得注意的是,特考韦瑞 和布西多福韦在 SEVI 增强条件下保留了活性。这些发现将精液淀粉样蛋白确定为 宿主 因子,可放大 MPXV 感染性并扩大细胞向性,表明精液积极促进 猴痘 的性 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 是由猴痘病毒(MPXV)引起的重新出现的 人兽共患病,最近的 暴发 是由性 传播 驱动的。然而,精液如何导致 MPXV 感染仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究调查了人精液如何增强易感靶细胞(包括原代人成纤维细胞、角质形成细胞、上皮细胞、原代单核细胞和离体人皮肤)中 MPXV 的感染。
核心方法
对来自多个捐赠者的精液进行 MPXV 预暴露,并测量感染增强情况。评估了精液衍生肽片段组装成阳离子淀粉样蛋白原纤维(SEVI 和 SEM)的作用,并使用原纤维靶向分子镊子 (CLR01) 破坏精液淀粉样蛋白。
主要结果
MPXV 预先暴露于精液会显着增加原代人成纤维细胞和角质形成细胞的感染。SEVI 增强了上皮细胞、原代单核细胞和离体人体皮肤以及临床 MPXV 分离株和其他 正痘病毒 的感染。SEVI 增加了病毒粒子的附着和摄取,而 CLR01 破坏了精液淀粉样蛋白并消除了增强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,精液淀粉样蛋白充当 宿主 因子,放大 MPXV 感染性并扩大细胞向性,表明精液积极促进 猴痘 的性 传播。这确定了精液增强 MPXV 感染的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对精液淀粉样蛋白可能是减少 猴痘 性 传播 的策略。特考韦瑞 和布西多福韦在 SEVI 增强条件下保留的活性支持它们在 治疗 中的继续使用。
局限与证据强度
该研究证明了体外和离体感染增强的明确机制,但需要进一步的体内验证。由于使用了多个供体样本、原代细胞和临床分离株,证据强度很高。

Seminal fluid enhances monkeypox virus infection via amyloidogenic fibrils

Authors: Emma Torbica, Matthias Denkewitz, Tamara Quick, Nadja Zöller, Katja Steinhorst, Stefan Kippenberger, Florian Rothweiler, Jindrich Cinatl, Pia Veratti, Florian Full
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is a re-emerging zoonosis caused by monkeypox virus (MPXV), with recent outbreaks driven by sexual transmission. How semen contributes to MPXV infection, however, remains unclear. Here, we show that human seminal fluid potently enhances MPXV infection in susceptible target cells. Pre-exposure of MPXV to seminal fluid from multiple donors markedly increased infection of primary human fibroblasts and keratinocytes. Semen-derived peptide fragments that assemble into cationic amyloid fibrils (SEVI and SEM) enhanced infection of epithelial cells, primary monocytes, and ex vivo human skin, as well as clinical MPXV isolates and other orthopoxviruses . SEVI increased virion attachment and uptake, and fibril-targeting molecular tweezer (CLR01) disrupted seminal amyloids and abrogated enhancement. Notably, tecovirimat and brincidofovir retained activity under SEVI-enhanced conditions. These findings identify seminal amyloids as host factors that amplify MPXV infectivity and broaden cell tropism, suggesting that semen actively promotes sexual transmission of mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox is a re-emerging zoonosis caused by monkeypox virus (MPXV), with recent outbreaks driven by sexual transmission. How semen contributes to MPXV infection, however, remains unclear.
Design and population
The study investigated how human seminal fluid enhances MPXV infection in susceptible target cells, including primary human fibroblasts, keratinocytes, epithelial cells, primary monocytes, and ex vivo human skin.
Core methods
Pre-exposure of MPXV to seminal fluid from multiple donors was performed, and infection enhancement was measured. The role of semen-derived peptide fragments assembling into cationic amyloid fibrils (SEVI and SEM) was assessed, and fibril-targeting molecular tweezer (CLR01) was used to disrupt seminal amyloids.
Main results
Pre-exposure of MPXV to seminal fluid markedly increased infection of primary human fibroblasts and keratinocytes. SEVI enhanced infection of epithelial cells, primary monocytes, and ex vivo human skin, as well as clinical MPXV isolates and other orthopoxviruses. SEVI increased virion attachment and uptake, and CLR01 disrupted seminal amyloids and abrogated enhancement.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that seminal amyloids act as host factors that amplify MPXV infectivity and broaden cell tropism, suggesting that semen actively promotes sexual transmission of mpox. This identifies a novel mechanism by which semen enhances MPXV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that targeting seminal amyloids could be a strategy to reduce sexual transmission of mpox. The retained activity of tecovirimat and brincidofovir under SEVI-enhanced conditions supports their continued use in treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates a clear mechanism of infection enhancement in vitro and ex vivo, but further in vivo validation is needed. The evidence strength is high due to the use of multiple donor samples, primary cells, and clinical isolates.

了解 猴痘 黑人和拉丁裔男同性恋和双性恋男性的知识、沟通和护理参与度

Understanding Mpox Knowledge, Communication, and Care Engagement Among Black and Latino Gay and Bisexual Men
作者： Megan T. Ebor, Maile Y. Karris, Jasmine Barnes, Kim E. Parker, Bhjan Kaur, Glavee Glavee, Jill Blumenthal, Chadwick K. Campbell
摘要中文翻译猴痘 (猴痘) 对同性恋和双性恋男性 (GBM) 的影响尤为严重,特别是黑人和拉丁裔社区,但有限的研究探讨了这些人群在 猴痘 期间如何理解和导航与 猴痘 相关的信息和护理。这项定性研究探讨了影响黑人和拉丁裔 GBM 的 猴痘 知识和医疗保健参与的健康沟通、信任和结构性因素。2023 年 11 月至 2024 年 5 月期间,我们对从加利福尼亚州圣地亚哥的临床样本中招募的黑人和拉丁裔 GBM 进行了 30 次半结构化访谈。访谈探讨了参与者的 猴痘 知识、信息来源、沟通实践和医疗保健经验。使用归纳和演绎编码策略的快速定性分析方法对数据进行分析。参与者描述了对 猴痘 的有限且不断发展的知识,信息主要通过社交媒体、同行网络和新闻媒体获得。猴痘 消息经常被认为是侮辱性的和基于身份的,塑造了易感性的看法并加剧了对歧视的担忧。同伴网络成为信息交流、正常化以及与预防和护理相关的支持的重要来源。参与者表示,在与被认为肯定、值得信赖且了解 LGBTQ+ 健康的提供者互动时,他们对护理的参与度更高,疫苗 的接受度也更高。LGBTQ+ 护理方面的提供者能力有限,削弱了参与医疗保健系统的信任和意愿。结构性不平等,包括种族主义、耻辱和获得医疗保健的障碍,进一步影响了参与者对 猴痘 预防的体验。研究结果强调了文化响应型公共卫生沟通、肯定临床护理以及社区知情策略的重要性,这些策略利用同伴网络支持黑人和拉丁裔 GBM 之间公平的 猴痘 预防和医疗保健参与。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决 猴痘 对黑人和拉丁裔同性恋和双性恋男性 (GBM) 的不成比例的影响,因为有限的研究调查了这些人群在 猴痘 期间如何理解和导航与 猴痘 相关的信息和护理。
设计与对象
这项定性研究于 2023 年 11 月至 2024 年 5 月期间进行,对从加利福尼亚州圣地亚哥的临床样本中招募的黑人和拉丁裔 GBM 进行了 30 次半结构化访谈。
核心方法
使用归纳和演绎编码策略的快速定性分析方法对数据进行分析,并通过访谈探索参与者的 猴痘 知识、信息来源、沟通实践和医疗保健经验。
主要结果
参与者描述了对 猴痘 的有限且不断发展的知识,信息主要通过社交媒体、同行网络和新闻媒体获得;猴痘 消息经常被认为是侮辱性的和基于身份的,塑造了易感性的看法并加剧了对歧视的担忧。
解释与创新
作者解释说,同伴网络已成为与预防和护理相关的信息交换、正常化和支持的重要来源,并且文化上敏感的公共卫生沟通和肯定临床护理对于公平的 猴痘 预防和卫生保健参与至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明了利用同伴网络和实施文化响应型公共卫生战略的重要性,以支持黑人和拉丁裔 GBM 之间公平的 猴痘 预防和医疗保健参与,并强调了对 LGBTQ+ 护理提供者能力的需求。
局限与证据强度
由于定性研究设计,证据强度中等,限制了普遍性,尽管研究结果为塑造 猴痘 体验的结构性不平等和医疗保健获取障碍提供了宝贵的见解。

Understanding Mpox Knowledge, Communication, and Care Engagement Among Black and Latino Gay and Bisexual Men

Authors: Megan T. Ebor, Maile Y. Karris, Jasmine Barnes, Kim E. Parker, Bhjan Kaur, Glavee Glavee, Jill Blumenthal, Chadwick K. Campbell
Original abstract (English)
The monkeypox (Mpox) disproportionately impacted gay and bisexual men (GBM), particularly Black and Latino communities, yet limited research has examined how these populations understood and navigated Mpox-related information and care during the outbreak. This qualitative study explored health communication, trust, and structural factors influencing Mpox knowledge and health care engagement among Black and Latino GBM. Between November 2023 and May 2024, we conducted 30 semi-structured interviews with Black and Latino GBM recruited from a clinic-based sample in San Diego, California. Interviews explored participants’ Mpox knowledge, information sources, communication practices, and health care experiences. Data were analyzed using a rapid qualitative analysis approach informed by inductive and deductive coding strategies. Participants described limited and evolving knowledge of Mpox, with information obtained primarily through social media, peer networks, and news media. Mpox messaging was frequently perceived as stigmatizing and identity-based, shaping perceptions of susceptibility and contributing to concerns about discrimination. Peer networks emerged as critical sources of information exchange, normalization, and support related to prevention and care. Participants reported stronger engagement in care and vaccine uptake when interacting with providers perceived as affirming, trustworthy, and knowledgeable about LGBTQ+ health. Limited provider competency in LGBTQ+ care undermined trust and willingness to engage health care systems. Structural inequities, including racism, stigma, and barriers to health care access, further shaped participants’ experiences with Mpox prevention. Findings underscore the importance of culturally responsive public health communication, affirming clinical care, and community-informed strategies that leverage peer networks to support equitable Mpox prevention and health care engagement among Black and Latino GBM.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the disproportionate impact of Mpox on Black and Latino gay and bisexual men (GBM), as limited research had examined how these populations understood and navigated Mpox-related information and care during the outbreak.
Design and population
This qualitative study was conducted between November 2023 and May 2024, involving 30 semi-structured interviews with Black and Latino GBM recruited from a clinic-based sample in San Diego, California.
Core methods
Data were analyzed using a rapid qualitative analysis approach informed by inductive and deductive coding strategies, with interviews exploring participants' Mpox knowledge, information sources, communication practices, and health care experiences.
Main results
Participants described limited and evolving knowledge of Mpox, with information obtained primarily through social media, peer networks, and news media; Mpox messaging was frequently perceived as stigmatizing and identity-based, shaping perceptions of susceptibility and contributing to concerns about discrimination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that peer networks emerged as critical sources of information exchange, normalization, and support related to prevention and care, and that culturally responsive public health communication and affirming clinical care are essential for equitable Mpox prevention and health care engagement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of leveraging peer networks and implementing culturally responsive public health strategies to support equitable Mpox prevention and health care engagement among Black and Latino GBM, highlighting the need for provider competency in LGBTQ+ care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the qualitative study design, which limits generalizability, though the findings provide valuable insights into structural inequities and health care access barriers shaping Mpox experiences.

猴痘 上科学生产的全球趋势:文献计量回顾

Global Trends in Scientific Production on Mpox: A Bibliometric Review
作者： Francisco Ocian de Araúo Junior, Marcia Helena Machado Nascimento, Crislen de Melo Conceição, Thiago Marcírio Gonçalves de Castro, Gisele Maria Cardoso da Silva, Neiva José da Luz Dias Junior, Laryssa Cristiane Palheta Vulcão, Raisa Oksana Lídia Ellis Freire de Sena Garcia da Silva, Adriana de Sá Pinheiro dos Santos, Camila Cristina Girard Santos
摘要中文翻译背景:/目标:分析 猴痘 上全球科学成果的概况,突出显示不同知识领域的作者、来源、关键词和趋势。方法:2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 3 月期间使用 Web of Science、Scopus、Cochrane 和 PubMed 数据库进行的文献计量研究,除了科学制图之外,还应用了 Bradford 定律、Lotka 定律和 Zipf 定律。结果:共识别出 1,146 篇文档,其中以期刊文章为主(724 篇;62.9%)。根据布拉德福德定律,22 种期刊集中了 381 篇(33.2%)出版物。Lotka 定律揭示了作者的集中度,一位作者负责 33 篇文章(0.5%)。根据齐普夫定律,47 个(0.74%)单词构成了最频繁的“琐碎区域”。共同作者和关键词映射展示了涉及公共卫生、病毒学和 流行病学 的跨学科特征。结论:与预测因素、传播、疫苗 和 治疗 相关的研究取得了显着进展,并且数学模型的使用也越来越多。然而,在具体疗法、长期影响和有效的预防措施方面仍然存在差距,这凸显出需要进一步调查以确保对未来的 暴发 做出快速反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描绘 猴痘 上的全球科学成果,以确定不同知识领域的作者、来源、关键词和趋势,满足快速发展的研究成果中的综合需求。
设计与对象
2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 3 月期间,利用 Web of Science、Scopus、Cochrane 和 PubMed 数据库进行了一项文献计量研究,应用布拉德福德定律、洛特卡定律和齐普夫定律以及科学制图。
核心方法
该分析应用布拉德福德定律、洛特卡定律和齐普夫定律分别评估期刊集中度、作者生产力和关键词频率,并使用共同作者和关键词映射来评估跨学科研究模式。
主要结果
共识别出 1,146 篇文档,其中 724 篇(62.9%)为期刊文章,22 种期刊集中了 381 篇(33.2%)出版物,一名作者负责 33 篇(0.5%)篇文章。Zipf 定律显示 47 个 (0.74%) 个单词构成了最常见的“琐碎区域”,而共同作者和关键词映射则揭示了公共卫生、病毒学和 流行病学 等领域的跨学科参与。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 猴痘 研究的重大进展,特别是在预测因素、传播、疫苗 和 治疗 方面,以及数学模型的使用增加,同时强调了特定疗法、长期影响和预防措施方面持续存在的差距。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了协调研究工作和有针对性的调查的重要性,以解决 猴痘 治疗和预防策略的差距,为未来的 监测、研究重点和 暴发 应对计划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据因其对文献计量方法和预印本数据的依赖而受到限制,这些方法和预印本数据可能无法涵盖正在进行的研究的全部范围或反映纳入研究的质量,因此需要对研究结果进行谨慎解释。

Global Trends in Scientific Production on Mpox: A Bibliometric Review

Authors: Francisco Ocian de Araúo Junior, Marcia Helena Machado Nascimento, Crislen de Melo Conceição, Thiago Marcírio Gonçalves de Castro, Gisele Maria Cardoso da Silva, Neiva José da Luz Dias Junior, Laryssa Cristiane Palheta Vulcão, Raisa Oksana Lídia Ellis Freire de Sena Garcia da Silva, Adriana de Sá Pinheiro dos Santos, Camila Cristina Girard Santos
Original abstract (English)
Background: /Objectives: To analyze the profile of global scientific production on Mpox, highlighting authors, sources, keywords, and trends across different fields of knowledge. Methods: A bibliometric study conducted between December 2024 and March 2025 using the Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane, and PubMed databases, applying Bradford’s, Lotka’s, and Zipf’s laws, in addition to scientific mapping. Results: A total of 1,146 documents were identified, with a predominance of journal articles (724; 62.9%). According to Bradford’s Law, 22 journals concentrated 381 (33.2%) publications. Lotka’s Law revealed author concentration, with one author responsible for 33 (0.5%) articles. Based on Zipf’s Law, 47 (0.74%) words comprised the most frequent “trivial zone.” Co-authorship and keyword mapping demonstrated an interdisciplinary character involving public health, virology, and epidemiology. Conclusions: There was notable progress in research related to predictive factors, transmission, vaccines, and treatments, as well as a growing use of mathematical models. However, gaps remain regarding specific therapies, long-term impacts, and effective preventive measures, underscoring the need for further investigations to ensure rapid responses to future outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to profile global scientific production on mpox to identify authors, sources, keywords, and trends across different fields of knowledge, addressing the need for synthesis amid rapidly evolving research output.
Design and population
A bibliometric study was conducted between December 2024 and March 2025 using the Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane, and PubMed databases, applying Bradford’s, Lotka’s, and Zipf’s laws alongside scientific mapping.
Core methods
The analysis applied Bradford’s, Lotka’s, and Zipf’s laws to assess journal concentration, author productivity, and keyword frequency, respectively, and used co-authorship and keyword mapping to evaluate interdisciplinary research patterns.
Main results
A total of 1,146 documents were identified, with 724 (62.9%) being journal articles, 22 journals concentrating 381 (33.2%) publications, and one author responsible for 33 (0.5%) articles. Zipf’s Law showed 47 (0.74%) words comprising the most frequent “trivial zone,” while co-authorship and keyword mapping revealed interdisciplinary involvement in public health, virology, and epidemiology.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating significant progress in mpox research, particularly in predictive factors, transmission, vaccines, and treatments, alongside increased use of mathematical models, while highlighting persistent gaps in specific therapies, long-term impacts, and preventive measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the importance of coordinated research efforts and targeted investigations to address gaps in mpox therapies and preventive strategies, informing future surveillance, research priorities, and outbreak response planning.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by its reliance on bibliometric methods and preprint data, which may not capture the full scope of ongoing research or reflect the quality of included studies, warranting cautious interpretation of the findings.

Journal: Virologica Sinica  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-23  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.virs.2026.07.009  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, S1995-820X(26)00122-7
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

MMF 通过破坏 IMPDH2 与 USP5 的相互作用并诱导其杆环组装来抑制痘病毒感染。

MMF inhibits poxvirus infection by disrupting IMPDH2 interaction with USP5 and inducing its Rod-and-Ring assemblies.
作者： Qian Sun, Kesen Liu, Wandi Cao, Chengyue Wu, Hanhua Zhang, Xingya Wang, Chen Peng, Jie Sun, Anbing Zhang, Zhuo Zhou
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 是一种致病性 正痘病毒,已引起严重的 暴发 并成为全球公共卫生威胁。尽管 抗病毒药物 被批准用于天花,可用于治疗猴痘,但其临床应用受到药物可用性和新出现的耐药性的限制。病毒重塑 宿主 核苷酸代谢以确保生物合成前体进行复制和传播的保守策略已成为开发广谱 抗病毒药物 疗法的关键目标。在本研究中,利用痘苗病毒(VACV)和MPXV之间高度的遗传和生物学相似性,我们采用VACV作为替代模型,筛选10种FDA批准的针对核苷酸代谢酶的抑制剂,旨在寻找潜在的针对MPXV的新型抑制剂。吗替麦考酚酯 (MMF) 是一种肌苷 5'-单磷酸脱氢酶 II 型 (IMPDH2) 抑制剂,对 VACV 和 MPXV 均表现出有效的抑制作用。随后的下游时间过程研究表明,MMF 针对病毒复制周期的进入后阶段。机制研究表明,MMF 通过抑制泛素特异性蛋白酶 5 (USP5) 介导的 IMPDH2 去泛素化并诱导杆环 (R&R) 组装来抑制 IMPDH2 活性,从而减少 dNTP 池并增强 抗病毒药物 效应。总之,我们的研究结果表明,MMF是一种有效的针对VACV和MPXV感染的抗病毒药物药物,并建立了针对宿主的治疗策略来对抗未来的正痘病毒 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒(MPXV)引起了严重的 暴发 疾病,对全球公共卫生构成威胁,但由于药物可用性和新出现的耐药性,批准用于天花的 抗病毒药物 的临床应用有限。该研究旨在通过靶向 宿主 核苷酸代谢来确定广谱 抗病毒药物 疗法,宿主 核苷酸代谢是病毒用来确保生物合成前体进行复制的保守策略。
设计与对象
该研究利用痘苗病毒(VACV)作为 MPXV 的替代模型,因为它们具有高度的遗传和生物学相似性,筛选了 10 种 FDA 批准的针对核苷酸代谢酶的抑制剂。该研究重点关注机械效应和 抗病毒药物 效应,但未指定样本量、参与者或研究日期。
核心方法
该研究筛选了 FDA 批准的针对核苷酸代谢酶的抑制剂,并确定吗替麦考酚酯 (MMF) 是 VACV 和 MPXV 的有效抑制剂。机理研究评估了 MMF 对 IMPDH2 活性、USP5 介导的去泛素化以及杆环 (R&R) 组装诱导的影响。
主要结果
MMF 对 VACV 和 MPXV 均表现出有效的抑制作用。时程研究表明,MMF 针对病毒复制周期的进入后阶段。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,MMF是一种有效对抗VACV和MPXV感染的抗病毒药物药物,建立了针对宿主的治疗策略,以对抗未来的正痘病毒 暴发。新颖之处在于确定了 MMF 的作用机制,涉及 IMPDH2 抑制和 R&R 组装诱导。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,MMF 可以作为 宿主 定向的 抗病毒药物 治疗剂,用于治疗 正痘病毒 感染(包括 MPXV),解决当前 治疗 选择的差距和耐药性挑战。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,由于药物可用性和耐药性,批准用于天花的 抗病毒药物 的临床实用性有限,这意味着 MMF 转化的潜在限制。证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于替代模型(VACV)并且缺乏直接的临床验证。

MMF inhibits poxvirus infection by disrupting IMPDH2 interaction with USP5 and inducing its Rod-and-Ring assemblies.

Authors: Qian Sun, Kesen Liu, Wandi Cao, Chengyue Wu, Hanhua Zhang, Xingya Wang, Chen Peng, Jie Sun, Anbing Zhang, Zhuo Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox virus (MPXV), a pathogenic orthopoxvirus, has caused major outbreaks and emerged as a global public health threat. Although antivirals approved for smallpox are used therapeutically against monkeypox, their clinical utility is limited by drug availability and emerging resistance. The conserved strategy by which viruses remodel host nucleotide metabolism to secure biosynthetic precursors for replication and spread has emerged as a pivotal target for the development of broad-spectrum antiviral therapeutics. In this study, leveraging the high genetic and biological similarity between vaccinia virus (VACV) and MPXV, we employed VACV as a surrogate model to screen 10 FDA-approved inhibitors targeting nucleotide metabolism enzymes, aiming to identify potential novel inhibitors against MPXV. Mycophenolate mofetil (MMF), an inosine 5'-monophosphate dehydrogenase type II (IMPDH2) inhibitor, displayed potent inhibition effects against both VACV and MPXV. Subsequent downstream time-course studies revealed that MMF targets a post-entry stage of the viral replication cycle. Mechanistic studies suggest that MMF inhibits IMPDH2 activity by suppressing ubiquitin-specific protease 5 (USP5)-mediated deubiquitination of IMPDH2 and inducing rod-and-ring (R&R) assembly, leading to reducing dNTP pools and enhancing antiviral effects. In conclusion, our findings demonstrate that MMF is an effective antiviral drug against VACV and MPXV infection and establish a host-directed therapeutic strategy to combat future orthopoxvirus outbreaks.

Question and background
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) has caused major outbreaks and is a global public health threat, yet antivirals approved for smallpox have limited clinical utility due to drug availability and emerging resistance. The study aimed to identify broad-spectrum antiviral therapeutics by targeting host nucleotide metabolism, a conserved strategy used by viruses to secure biosynthetic precursors for replication.
Design and population
The study used vaccinia virus (VACV) as a surrogate model for MPXV due to their high genetic and biological similarity, screening 10 FDA-approved inhibitors targeting nucleotide metabolism enzymes. The research focused on mechanistic and antiviral effects without specifying sample size, participants, or study dates.
Core methods
The study screened FDA-approved inhibitors targeting nucleotide metabolism enzymes and identified mycophenolate mofetil (MMF) as a potent inhibitor of both VACV and MPXV. Mechanistic investigations assessed MMF's effects on IMPDH2 activity, USP5-mediated deubiquitination, and induction of rod-and-ring (R&R) assemblies.
Main results
MMF displayed potent inhibition effects against both VACV and MPXV. Time-course studies revealed that MMF targets a post-entry stage of the viral replication cycle.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that MMF is an effective antiviral drug against VACV and MPXV infection, establishing a host-directed therapeutic strategy to combat future orthopoxvirus outbreaks. The novelty lies in identifying MMF's mechanism of action involving IMPDH2 inhibition and R&R assembly induction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest MMF could serve as a host-directed antiviral therapeutic for orthopoxvirus infections, including MPXV, addressing gaps in current treatment options and resistance challenges.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that antivirals approved for smallpox have limited clinical utility due to drug availability and resistance, implying potential constraints for MMF's translation. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on a surrogate model (VACV) and lacks direct clinical validation.

一种经过实验验证的基于结构的计算框架,用于抗 正痘病毒 抗体的人源化。

An experimentally validated structure-based computational framework for humanisation of anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies.
作者： Xuehua Yang, Xuemeng Dong, Jiahan Lu, Xiaojing Chi, Xiuying Liu, Huarui Duan, Peixiang Gao, Jing Xue, Wei Yang
摘要中文翻译背景:正痘病毒,尤其是 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 的重新出现,对全球公共卫生构成了日益严重的威胁。充分表征的鼠抗 正痘病毒 抗体在临床上受到抗小鼠抗体反应的限制,而传统的基于序列的人源化通常会损害抗原结合活性。方法:我们开发了一个经过实验验证的结构引导计算人性化框架,优先考虑 3D 结构一致性而不是序列同一性,整合基于 Foldseek 的结构比对和界面残基约束。我们应用该框架对两种鼠抗 正痘病毒 抗体(7D11、A27D7)进行人源化,并进行了全面的体外和体内验证。结果:结构叠加证实了人源化变体(POX1.1 和 POX2.1)与其亲本 mAb 之间的高度构象保守性,所有可变域的均方根偏差 (RMSD) 值低于 0.6 Å。两种人源化变体都保留了具有自然人性特征的完整表位特异性。与亲本 7D11 相比,POX1.1 对痘苗病毒 (VACV) 和 MPXV 的中和效力增强。POX2.1 保留了亲本 A27D7 的广泛交叉反应结合和细胞外包膜病毒粒子中和活性。在致死性 VACV 小鼠模型中,两种单一疗法均具有显着的预防和治疗保护作用,减少肺部病毒载量并提高生存率。与单独的抗体相比,POX1.1和POX2.1的双靶向组合实现了显着改善的体内功效,即使在攻击后2天施用,也能实现100%的存活率。在 MPXV CAST/EiJ 小鼠模型中,该组合显着减少了脾脏肿大以及血浆、脾脏和肺组织中的 MPXV DNA 载量,有效抑制了全身病毒传播。解释:这些研究结果表明,以结构为中心的工作流程能够有效地人源化特征良好的鼠抗 正痘病毒 抗体,从而提供了一个经过验证的框架来支持针对 正痘病毒 大流行的对策的开发。基金资助:该工作得到了国家自然科学基金委员会、中国医学科学院医学科学创新基金、青年教师科研创新能力支撑项目和国家科技重大专项的支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
正痘病毒(包括猴痘病毒(MPXV))的重新出现代表着日益严重的全球公共卫生威胁,现有的鼠抗正痘病毒抗体由于抗小鼠免疫应答而面临临床限制。
设计与对象
该研究开发并验证了一种结构引导的计算框架,用于人源化两种鼠抗 正痘病毒 抗体(7D11 和 A27D7),应用体外和体内评估来评估人源化变体 POX1.1 和 POX2.1。
核心方法
使用优先考虑 3D 结构一致性的结构引导计算人源化框架,整合基于 Foldseek 的结构比对和界面残基约束来人源化鼠抗体,然后进行全面的体外和体内验证。
主要结果
结构叠加显示人源化变体和亲本单克隆抗体之间具有高度构象保守性(RMSD < 0.6 Å),两种变体都保留了具有天然人性特征的完整表位特异性,并且与 7D11 相比,POX1.1 对痘苗病毒 (VACV) 和 MPXV 的中和作用增强。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,以结构为中心的工作流程可以实现鼠抗 正痘病毒 抗体的高效人源化,同时保留抗原结合活性,从而提供经过验证的框架来支持 正痘病毒 对策的开发。
科研与公卫意义
该框架通过解决鼠抗体的局限性并增强中和和保护功效,支持开发人源化抗体作为对抗 正痘病毒 感染(包括 猴痘)的对策。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于小鼠模型和体外测定,可能无法完全捕获人类 免疫应答 或临床疗效,表明需要在人体研究中进一步验证以确认转化潜力。

An experimentally validated structure-based computational framework for humanisation of anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies.

Authors: Xuehua Yang, Xuemeng Dong, Jiahan Lu, Xiaojing Chi, Xiuying Liu, Huarui Duan, Peixiang Gao, Jing Xue, Wei Yang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The re-emergence of orthopoxviruses, most notably mpox virus (MPXV), poses a growing global public health threat. Well-characterised murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies are clinically limited by anti-mouse antibody responses, while traditional sequence-based humanisation often impairs antigen-binding activity. METHODS: We developed an experimentally validated structure-guided computational humanisation framework prioritising 3D architectural congruence over sequence identity, integrating Foldseek-based structural alignment and interface-residue constraints. We applied this framework to humanise two murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies (7D11, A27D7), with comprehensive in vitro and in vivo validation. FINDINGS: Structural superimposition confirmed high conformational conservation between the humanised variants (POX1.1 and POX2.1) and their parental mAbs, with root mean square deviation (RMSD) values below 0.6 Å for all variable domains. Both humanised variants retained full epitope specificity with natural humanness profiles. POX1.1 showed enhanced neutralisation potency against vaccinia virus (VACV) and MPXV, compared with the parental 7D11. POX2.1 preserved the broad cross-reactive binding and the extracellular enveloped virion neutralising activity of the parental A27D7. In the lethal VACV mouse model, both monotherapies conferred significant prophylactic and therapeutic protection, reducing pulmonary viral loads and improving survival. The dual-targeting combination of POX1.1 and POX2.1 achieved markedly improved in vivo efficacy compared with individual antibodies, delivering 100% survival even when administered 2 days post-challenge. In the MPXV CAST/EiJ mouse model, the combination significantly reduced splenomegaly and MPXV DNA loads in plasma, spleen and lung tissues, effectively suppressing systemic viral dissemination. INTERPRETATION: These findings establish that the structure-centric workflow enables efficient humanisation of well-characterised murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies, providing a validated framework to support the development of countermeasures for orthopoxvirus pandemic. FUNDING: This work was supported by the National Natural Science Foundation of China, the Chinese Academy of Medical Sciences Innovation Fund for Medical Sciences, the Scientific Research Innovation Capability Support Project for Young Faculty and the National Science and Technology Major Project.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The re-emergence of orthopoxviruses, including mpox virus (MPXV), represents a growing global public health threat, and existing murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies face clinical limitations due to anti-mouse immune responses.
Design and population
The study developed and validated a structure-guided computational framework to humanise two murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies (7D11 and A27D7), applying in vitro and in vivo assessments to evaluate the humanised variants POX1.1 and POX2.1.
Core methods
A structure-guided computational humanisation framework prioritising 3D architectural congruence was used, integrating Foldseek-based structural alignment and interface-residue constraints to humanise the murine antibodies, followed by comprehensive in vitro and in vivo validation.
Main results
Structural superimposition showed high conformational conservation between humanised variants and parental mAbs (RMSD < 0.6 Å), both variants retained full epitope specificity with natural humanness profiles, and POX1.1 exhibited enhanced neutralisation against vaccinia virus (VACV) and MPXV compared with 7D11.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the structure-centric workflow enables efficient humanisation of murine anti-orthopoxvirus antibodies while preserving antigen-binding activity, providing a validated framework to support orthopoxvirus countermeasure development.
Scientific and public-health significance
This framework supports the development of humanised antibodies as countermeasures against orthopoxvirus infections, including mpox, by addressing limitations of murine antibodies and enhancing neutralisation and protective efficacy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on murine models and in vitro assays, which may not fully capture human immune responses or clinical efficacy, indicating a need for further validation in human studies to confirm translational potential.

用于猴痘推文基于方面的情感分析的混合 RoBERTa-BiLSTM 框架

A hybrid RoBERTa–BiLSTM framework for aspect-based sentiment analysis of Monkeypox tweets
作者： TDN Pavani, Kuppusamy P., Kuppusamy Pothanaicker
摘要中文翻译猴痘和COVID-19 暴发使社交媒体作为公共卫生信息的实时来源的重要性凸显出来,人们在此分享他们对不同健康问题的观点和担忧。然而,由于存在模糊语言和类别不平衡,可靠的情感提取任务变得困难。本文提出了一种混合模型,该模型使用RoBERTa和堆叠BiLSTM层进行基于方面的情感分析(ABSA)。所提出的模型利用了transformers表示上下文的能力以及BiLSTM学习序列依赖关系的能力,用于细粒度情感分类。所提出的方法在猴痘X(Twitter)大型数据集、通用COVID-19 Twitter语料库以及SemEval-2014 ABSA基准数据集上进行了严格测试,使用了准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、五折交叉验证和单因素方差分析测试。实验结果表明,所提出的方法在所有评估领域都优于传统机器学习、深度学习和基于transformer的基线模型,在猴痘数据集上达到95.17%的准确率和95.04%的F1分数,在COVID-19数据集上达到92.15%的准确率和91.87%的F1分数,在SemEval-2014数据集上达到87.68%的准确率和87.12%的F1分数。统计分析证明了在所有评估领域中性能改进的显著性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在猴痘和 COVID-19 等公共卫生 暴发 期间从社交媒体中可靠地提取方面级别情感的挑战,其中模糊的语言和类别不平衡使分析变得复杂。
设计与对象
本研究使用 Monkeypox X (Twitter) 和 COVID-19 Twitter 数据集以及 SemEval-2014 ABSA 基准开发和测试混合 RoBERTa-BiLSTM 模型,用于基于方面的情感分析。
核心方法
所提出的模型将基于 Transformer 的上下文表示与 BiLSTM 顺序依赖学习相结合,并使用准确度、精确度、召回率、F1 分数、五折交叉验证和单向方差分析测试进行评估。
主要结果
该混合模型在 Monkeypox 数据集上实现了 95.17% 的准确度和 95.04% F1 分数,在 COVID-19 数据集上实现了 92.15% 的准确度和 91.87% F1 分数,在 SemEval-2014 上实现了 87.68% 的准确度和 87.12% F1 分数,优于传统和基于 Transformer 的基线。
解释与创新
作者将混合方法解释为有效解决了公共卫生背景下细粒度情绪分类的现有方法的局限性,展示了跨多个领域的卓越性能。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过提高 暴发 相关社交媒体数据的情绪分析准确性来增强实时公共卫生监测,有助于了解健康危机期间公众的担忧和反应。
局限与证据强度
由于对多个数据集和统计验证进行了严格测试,证据强度很高,尽管局限性可能包括 Twitter 数据之外的潜在普遍性和特定领域的语言变化。

A hybrid RoBERTa–BiLSTM framework for aspect-based sentiment analysis of Monkeypox tweets

Authors: TDN Pavani, Kuppusamy P., Kuppusamy Pothanaicker
Original abstract (English)
The Monkeypox and COVID-19 outbreaks have brought into focus the significance of social media as a real-time source of public health information, where people share their opinions and concerns regarding different health issues. However, the task of reliable aspect-level sentiment extraction is difficult due to the presence of ambiguous language and class imbalance. This paper presents a hybrid model that uses RoBERTa and stacked BiLSTM layers for aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA). The proposed model utilizes the capability of transformers to represent context and the ability of BiLSTM to learn sequential dependencies for fine-grained sentiment classification. The proposed approach is rigorously tested on the primary Monkeypox X (Twitter) large-scale dataset, a generalization COVID-19 Twitter corpus, and the SemEval-2014 ABSA benchmark using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, five-fold cross-validation, and one-way ANOVA testing. The experimental results show that the proposed approach performs better than traditional machine learning, deep learning, and transformer-based baselines, achieving an accuracy of 95.17% and an F1-score of 95.04% on the Monkeypox dataset, 92.15% accuracy and 91.87% F1-score on the COVID-19 dataset, and 87.68% accuracy and 87.12% F1-score on the SemEval-2014 dataset. Statistical analysis proves the significance of the performance improvements across all evaluated domains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of reliable aspect-level sentiment extraction from social media during public health outbreaks like Monkeypox and COVID-19, where ambiguous language and class imbalance complicate analysis.
Design and population
This research develops and tests a hybrid RoBERTa–BiLSTM model for aspect-based sentiment analysis using Monkeypox X (Twitter) and COVID-19 Twitter datasets, along with the SemEval-2014 ABSA benchmark.
Core methods
The proposed model combines transformer-based context representation with BiLSTM sequential dependency learning, evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, five-fold cross-validation, and one-way ANOVA testing.
Main results
The hybrid model achieved 95.17% accuracy and 95.04% F1-score on the Monkeypox dataset, 92.15% accuracy and 91.87% F1-score on the COVID-19 dataset, and 87.68% accuracy and 87.12% F1-score on SemEval-2014, outperforming traditional and transformer-based baselines.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the hybrid approach as effectively addressing the limitations of existing methods for fine-grained sentiment classification in public health contexts, demonstrating superior performance across multiple domains.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can enhance real-time public health monitoring by improving sentiment analysis accuracy for outbreak-related social media data, aiding in understanding public concerns and responses during health crises.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high due to rigorous testing with multiple datasets and statistical validation, though limitations may include potential generalizability beyond Twitter data and domain-specific language variations.

通过模拟和可解释的图卷积网络比较 猴痘 流行病控制:刚果共和国和尼日利亚的案例研究

Comparing Mpox epidemic controls through simulations and explainable graph convolution networks: A case study in the Republic of the Congo and Nigeria
作者： Francesco Branda, Giancarlo Ceccarelli, Massimo Ciccozzi, Fabio Scarpa, Ugo Lomoio, Francesco Chiodo, Pierangelo Veltri, Pietro Hiram Guzzi, M Ciccozzi
摘要中文翻译本研究通过应用图卷积网络(k-GCN 模型)研究了 猴痘 暴发 的动态,重点关注两个流行病学不同的非洲背景:刚果共和国(地方性分支 I,人畜共患 传播)和尼日利亚(重新出现的分支 II,人与人之间的 传播)。我们使用分区模型进行了模拟,该模型集成了基于图形的联系网络和每个国家特定的流行病学参数。结果表明,k-GCN 模型捕获了模拟图组织数据中的相关时间和结构依赖性。该研究支持将基于接触的模型与可解释的图神经网络相结合进行基于场景的流行病分析的潜在用途。所提出的方法可能有助于比较干预策略并为公共卫生决策生成证据,前提是结果在模型的假设和校准限制内进行解释。系统的审查。1725–1735 - Cuomo-Dannenburg G、McCain K、McCabe R、Unwin HJT、Doohan P、Nash RK、Hicks JT、Charniga K、Geismar C、Lambert B 等人 (2024) 马尔堡病毒病 暴发、数学模型和疾病参数:系统评价。摘要 猴痘 是一种日益受到全球关注的人畜共患疾病,由于其 猴痘 动态的复杂性,给公共卫生带来了重大挑战。在最近的全球健康危机的背景下,对建模和控制 暴发 的创新工具的需求变得越来越明显。评估了五种干预方案,从不干预到组合 疫苗接种、检疫和啮齿动物扑杀。然后训练 k-GCN 模型,根据模拟数据和可解释性来预测下一年的节点类别。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 是一种日益受到全球关注的人畜共患疾病,由于其 猴痘 动态的复杂性,给公共卫生带来了重大挑战。在最近的全球健康危机的背景下,对建模和控制 暴发 的创新工具的需求变得越来越明显。
设计与对象
本研究通过应用图卷积网络(k-GCN 模型)研究了 猴痘 暴发 的动态,重点关注两个流行病学不同的非洲背景:刚果共和国(地方性分支 I,人畜共患 传播)和尼日利亚(重新出现的分支 II,人与人之间的 传播)。
核心方法
我们使用分区模型进行了模拟,该模型集成了基于图形的联系网络和每个国家特定的流行病学参数。然后训练 k-GCN 模型以根据模拟数据预测下一年的节点类别,并使用可解释性技术(显着性图和集成梯度)来识别最有影响力的 传播 路径。
主要结果
结果表明,k-GCN 模型捕获了模拟图组织数据中的相关时间和结构依赖性。在模拟情景中,包括有针对性的 疫苗接种、隔离和 储存宿主 定向啮齿动物控制在内的组合策略最大程度地减少了人类感染和流行病负担。
解释与创新
该研究支持将基于接触的模型与可解释的图神经网络相结合进行基于场景的流行病分析的潜在用途。可解释性分析表明,人类与啮齿动物的相互作用在流行早期阶段具有影响力,随着时间的推移,人与啮齿动物之间的相互作用逐渐转向人与人之间的 传播。
科研与公卫意义
所提出的方法可能有助于比较干预策略并为公共卫生决策生成证据,前提是结果在模型的假设和校准限制内进行解释。这可以为不同流行病学环境下 猴痘 控制的有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该分析完全基于从开放获取来源检索的公开匿名数据,特别是 猴痘 上的世界卫生组织仪表板以及从同行评审文献中提取的数据。证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于模拟数据和模型假设,这可能会限制对现实世界场景的普遍适用性。

Comparing Mpox epidemic controls through simulations and explainable graph convolution networks: A case study in the Republic of the Congo and Nigeria

Authors: Francesco Branda, Giancarlo Ceccarelli, Massimo Ciccozzi, Fabio Scarpa, Ugo Lomoio, Francesco Chiodo, Pierangelo Veltri, Pietro Hiram Guzzi, M Ciccozzi
Original abstract (English)
This study investigates the dynamics of mpox outbreaks through the application of Graph Convolutional Networks (k-GCN model), focusing on two epidemiologically distinct African contexts: the Republic of the Congo (endemic Clade I, zoonotic transmission) and Nigeria (re-emerging Clade II, human-to-human transmission). We conducted simulations using a compartmental model that integrates graph-based contact networks and epidemiological parameters specific to each country. The results indicate that the k-GCN model captures relevant temporal and structural dependencies in the simulated graph-organized data. The study supports the potential usefulness of combining contact-based models with explainable graph neural networks for scenario-based epidemic analysis. The proposed approach may help compare intervention strategies and generate evidence for public health decision-making, provided that results are interpreted within the assumptions and calibration limits of the model. a systematic review. 1725–1735 - Cuomo-Dannenburg G, McCain K, McCabe R, Unwin HJT, Doohan P, Nash RK, Hicks JT, Charniga K, Geismar C, Lambert B et al (2024) Marburg virus disease outbreaks, mathematical models, and disease parameters: a systematic review. Abstract Mpox, a zoonotic disease of growing global concern, presents significant public health challenges because of the complexity of its transmission dynamics. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox, a zoonotic disease of growing global concern, presents significant public health challenges because of the complexity of its transmission dynamics. The need for innovative tools to model and control outbreaks has become increasingly evident in the context of recent global health crises.
Design and population
This study investigates the dynamics of mpox outbreaks through the application of Graph Convolutional Networks (k-GCN model), focusing on two epidemiologically distinct African contexts: the Republic of the Congo (endemic Clade I, zoonotic transmission) and Nigeria (re-emerging Clade II, human-to-human transmission).
Core methods
We conducted simulations using a compartmental model that integrates graph-based contact networks and epidemiological parameters specific to each country. A k-GCN model was then trained to predict node classes for the following year based on simulation data, and explainability techniques (saliency maps and integrated gradients) were used to identify the most influential transmission pathways.
Main results
The results indicate that the k-GCN model captures relevant temporal and structural dependencies in the simulated graph-organized data. Among the simulated scenarios, combined strategies including targeted vaccination, quarantine, and reservoir-directed rodent control produced the largest reductions in human infections and epidemic burden.
Interpretation and novelty
The study supports the potential usefulness of combining contact-based models with explainable graph neural networks for scenario-based epidemic analysis. Explainability analysis suggested that human-rodent interactions were influential during the early epidemic phase, with a gradual shift toward human-to-human transmission over time.
Scientific and public-health significance
The proposed approach may help compare intervention strategies and generate evidence for public health decision-making, provided that results are interpreted within the assumptions and calibration limits of the model. This could inform targeted public health interventions for mpox control in different epidemiological settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The analysis was conducted entirely on publicly available, anonymized data retrieved from open-access sources, specifically the World Health Organization dashboard on mpox and data extracted from peer-reviewed literature. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on simulated data and model assumptions, which may limit generalizability to real-world scenarios.

与直肠炎相关的急性 猴痘 的独特炎症免疫特征

Distinct Inflammatory Immune Signature in Acute Mpox Associated with Proctitis
作者： Joanne Byrne, Gurvin Saini, Alejandro Garcia-Leon, Dana Alalwan, Alan Landay, Liem Binh Luong Nguyen, Stefano Savinelli, Cathal O’Broin, Mary Horgan, Christine Kelly
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
进化枝 IIb 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 感染的免疫发病机制及其与临床严重程度的关系仍不清楚。该研究旨在表征急性和恢复期 猴痘 的细胞因子反应,以描绘炎症特征。
设计与对象
在多中心 队列研究 中,我们定量了三个成人组的 45 种血浆蛋白生物标志物;急性 猴痘(感染后 30 天采样的 PCR 确认的 IIb 分支 MPXV 感染)和未感染的对照在年龄、性别、种族和 HIV 状况方面相匹配。
核心方法
主成分分析和无监督层次聚类定义了免疫特征。使用逻辑回归探讨与临床特征的关联。
主要结果
Sixteen participants with acute mpox (median 6 [4–8] days from symptom-onset; 13% vaccinated) were compared to 16 controls. Three immune clusters were identified, with mucosal involvement associated with the inflammatory cluster 1 (OR 7.85, 95% CI 1.02-102.40; p=0.048).
解释与创新
急性进化枝 IIb 猴痘 显示出独特的炎症特征,全身炎症集中在粘膜疾病患者中。恢复期显示部分消退,但组织修复活性持续存在。
科研与公卫意义
这些途径可能支持对粘膜受累的 猴痘 患者进行风险分层和有针对性的治疗干预。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小(16 名急性 猴痘 参与者),由于特定的分支 IIb 焦点,因此普遍性有限。由于观察设计和较小的 队列研究,证据强度中等。

Distinct Inflammatory Immune Signature in Acute Mpox Associated with Proctitis

Authors: Joanne Byrne, Gurvin Saini, Alejandro Garcia-Leon, Dana Alalwan, Alan Landay, Liem Binh Luong Nguyen, Stefano Savinelli, Cathal O’Broin, Mary Horgan, Christine Kelly
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The immunopathogenesis of clade IIb Monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection and its relationship with clinical severity remains poorly defined. We characterised cytokine responses in acute and convalescent mpox to delineate inflammatory profiles. Methods In a multi-centre cohort, we quantified 45 plasma protein biomarkers in three adult groups; acute mpox (PCR-confirmed clade IIb MPXV infection sampled 30 days post infection) and uninfected controls matched for age, sex, race and HIV status. Biomarkers spanned innate and adaptive immune activation, systemic inflammation and tissue repair. Principal component analysis and unsupervised hierarchical clustering defined immune profiles. Associations with clinical features were explored using logistic regression. Results Sixteen participants with acute mpox (median 6 [4–8] days from symptom-onset; 13% vaccinated) were compared to 16 controls. Three immune clusters were identified. Cluster 1 comprised only unvaccinated acute mpox and exhibited a broad inflammatory signature (IFN-γ, CXCL9/10/11, CCL 7/8, IL-6/10, OSM). Cluster 2 displayed elevated TNFSF12 and FLT3LG, suggestive of tissue-remodelling. Cluster 3 demonstrated lower cytokine expression. Mucosal involvement was associated with the inflammatory cluster 1 (OR 7.85, 95% CI 1.02-102.40; p=0.048). Among 25 convalescent participants (median 405 [225-450] days post infection; 12% vaccinated), clustering did not distinguish cases from controls. However, targeted comparisons demonstrated persistent low-grade immune activation (IL-6, OSM) and tissue-remodelling signatures (TGF-α, VEGF-A). Conclusions Acute clade IIb mpox displays distinct inflammatory profiles, with systemic inflammation concentrated among those with mucosal disease. Convalescence shows partial resolution but persistent tissue-repair activity. These pathways may support risk stratification and targeted therapeutic interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The immunopathogenesis of clade IIb Monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection and its relationship with clinical severity remains poorly defined. The study aimed to characterise cytokine responses in acute and convalescent mpox to delineate inflammatory profiles.
Design and population
In a multi-centre cohort, we quantified 45 plasma protein biomarkers in three adult groups; acute mpox (PCR-confirmed clade IIb MPXV infection sampled 30 days post infection) and uninfected controls matched for age, sex, race and HIV status.
Core methods
Principal component analysis and unsupervised hierarchical clustering defined immune profiles. Associations with clinical features were explored using logistic regression.
Main results
Sixteen participants with acute mpox (median 6 [4–8] days from symptom-onset; 13% vaccinated) were compared to 16 controls. Three immune clusters were identified, with mucosal involvement associated with the inflammatory cluster 1 (OR 7.85, 95% CI 1.02-102.40; p=0.048).
Interpretation and novelty
Acute clade IIb mpox displays distinct inflammatory profiles, with systemic inflammation concentrated among those with mucosal disease. Convalescence shows partial resolution but persistent tissue-repair activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These pathways may support risk stratification and targeted therapeutic interventions for mpox patients with mucosal involvement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a small sample size (16 acute mpox participants) and limited generalisability due to the specific clade IIb focus. Evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and small cohort.

使用变阶刘维尔-卡普托分数阶导数的猴痘新数学模型

A novel mathematical model for monkeypox using variable-order Liouville–Caputo fractional derivatives
作者： Pushpendra Kumar, Vedat Suat Erturk, Vedat Suat Ertürk
摘要中文翻译在本文中,我们使用变阶 V1 型和 V2 型 Liouville-Caputo 分数阶导数研究猴痘的非线性模型。我们证明了所提出模型的解的存在性和唯一性。我们通过使用最近的预测校正方法得出数值解。我们为所提出的猴痘模型数值模拟提供了图形分析。给定的数值方法和在不同分数阶下获得的动态输出为所提出的猴痘模型增添了显着的新颖性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用变阶刘维尔-卡普托分数导数开发一种新的猴痘数学模型,以满足了解疾病动态时对先进分析工具的需求。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注猴痘的非线性数学模型,不涉及特定人群或临床 队列研究,因为它是一项理论研究。
核心方法
研究人员使用最近的预测校正方法得出了数值解,并证明了所提出模型的解的存在性和唯一性。
主要结果
该研究为猴痘模型的数值模拟提供了图形分析,并通过数值方法和在不同分数阶下获得的动态输出证明了显着的新颖性。
解释与创新
作者将变阶分数导数解释为猴痘建模的重大进步,强调了数值方法和动态输出的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的猴痘 暴发 预测模型提供信息,从而有助于 监测 和干预策略。
局限与证据强度
尽管严格的数学方法提供了坚实的基础,但由于其理论性质和缺乏实证验证,该研究的证据强度中等。

A novel mathematical model for monkeypox using variable-order Liouville–Caputo fractional derivatives

Authors: Pushpendra Kumar, Vedat Suat Erturk, Vedat Suat Ertürk
Original abstract (English)
In this article, we study a nonlinear model of monkeypox using variable-order V1-type and V2-type Liouville–Caputo fractional derivatives. We prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution for the proposed model. We derive the numerical solution by using a recent predictor-corrector method. We provide graphical analysis for the proposed numerical simulations of the monkeypox model. The given numerical methodology and the dynamical outputs obtained at different fractional orders add significant novelty to the proposed monkeypox model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a novel mathematical model for monkeypox using variable-order Liouville–Caputo fractional derivatives to address the need for advanced analytical tools in understanding the disease's dynamics.
Design and population
The research focused on a nonlinear mathematical model of monkeypox, with no specific population or clinical cohort involved, as it was a theoretical study.
Core methods
The researchers derived a numerical solution using a recent predictor-corrector method and proved the existence and uniqueness of the solution for the proposed model.
Main results
The study provided graphical analysis for the numerical simulations of the monkeypox model and demonstrated significant novelty through the numerical methodology and dynamical outputs obtained at different fractional orders.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the variable-order fractional derivatives as a significant advancement in modeling monkeypox, emphasizing the novelty of the numerical methodology and dynamical outputs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of more accurate predictive models for monkeypox outbreaks, aiding in surveillance and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to its theoretical nature and lack of empirical validation, though the rigorous mathematical approach provides a solid foundation.

埃塞俄比亚的社区知识、风险认知和 猴痘 的决定因素:扩展并行过程模型的应用

Community knowledge, risk perceptions, and determinants of mpox in Ethiopia: An application of the Extended Parallel Process Model
作者： Yibeyin Mulualem, Mohammed Hasen Badeso, Melaku Abebe, Aemro Yibeltal, Ketema Misganaw, Ebsa File, Yihalem Adamu, Yonas Hailu, Ikram Faris, Girma Hailamariam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 仍然是非洲的公共卫生威胁,埃塞俄比亚于 2025 年 5 月 26 日宣布了 猴痘。然而,关于社区对 猴痘 的了解和看法的证据有限,这对于定制行为干预措施至关重要。
设计与对象
2025 年,埃塞俄比亚进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究,采用多阶段系统随机抽样,采访了除卫生工作者家庭之外的成年家庭成员(> 18 岁)。
核心方法
使用采访者管理的有关社会人口统计学、猴痘 知识和感知威胁的问卷收集数据,并通过 SPSS v26 使用描述性和推论性统计(方差分析、逻辑回归)进行分析。
主要结果
只有 24.6% (192/782) 具有良好的 猴痘 知识,积极感知威胁为 45.1%,积极功效为 79.9% (625/782)。威胁-功效交互显着预测知识(p<0.001,ηp2=0.022),教育水平和职业等因素显示出显着关联。
解释与创新
作者将低 猴痘 知识解释为表明需要强有力的公共卫生干预措施,感知威胁和功效相互作用以形成知识,这是扩展并行过程模型在这种情况下的新颖应用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,干预措施应解决知识差距,将感知到的威胁与有效性相结合,并利用大众媒体和社交媒体作为首选信息来源(分别为 62.4% 和 23.3%)。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计限制了因果推断,而仅抽象的证据限制了对潜在偏见的评估;然而,大样本量和系统抽样为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

Community knowledge, risk perceptions, and determinants of mpox in Ethiopia: An application of the Extended Parallel Process Model

Authors: Yibeyin Mulualem, Mohammed Hasen Badeso, Melaku Abebe, Aemro Yibeltal, Ketema Misganaw, Ebsa File, Yihalem Adamu, Yonas Hailu, Ikram Faris, Girma Hailamariam
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Mpox remains a public health threat in Africa, affecting many countries. Ethiopia declared an mpox outbreak on May 26, 2025. Understanding community knowledge and perception is vital for tailoring mpox behavioural interventions. However, there is limited evidence on community knowledge and perceptions of mpox. The Extended Parallel Process Model provides a comprehensive framework for understanding both perceived threat and perceived efficacy. Therefore, this study assessed community knowledge and perceptions of mpox using the Extended Parallel Process Model in Ethiopia, 2025. Methods: A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted in Ethiopia using multi-stage systematic random sampling. Adult household members (>18 years) were interviewed; health worker households were excluded. Data were collected using interviewer-administered questionnaires on socio-demographics, mpox knowledge, and perceived threat. We used the Extended Parallel Process Model (EPPM) to assess perceived threat and perceived efficacy. Data were analysed with descriptive and inferential statistics (ANOVA, logistic regression) using SPSS v26. Results: The median age was 35 years (IQR: 28–44), and 50.8% (397/782) were male. Only 24.6% (192/782) had good mpox knowledge. Positive perceived threat was 45.1%, and positive efficacy was 79.9% (625/782). Threat-efficacy interaction significantly predicted knowledge (p<0.001, ηp2=0.022); positive threat (AOR=1.73, 95%CI:1.21-2.47), can’t read/write (AOR = 0.163; 95% CI: 0.046-0.579), read and write on formal education (AOR = 0.192; 95% CI: 0.074-0.501), farmers (AOR = 0.199; 95% CI: 0.079-0.501), and housewife (AOR = 0.274; 95% CI: 0.138-0.543). The preferred sources of information were mainstream media at 62.4% (488/782), followed by social media at 23.3% (182/782). Conclusions: The Mpox knowledge among the community was low, implying a need for robust public health interventions. Factors such as educational level, occupation, and perceived threat had a significant association with the mpox knowledge of the community. The perceived threat and perceived efficacy interact to shape mpox knowledge. It is recommended that interventions address knowledge and perception gaps, especially considering interventions that reach all audiences, pairing perceived threat with perceived efficacy, and leveraging mass and social media.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox remains a public health threat in Africa, and Ethiopia declared an outbreak on May 26, 2025. However, there is limited evidence on community knowledge and perceptions of mpox, which is vital for tailoring behavioural interventions.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted in Ethiopia in 2025 using multi-stage systematic random sampling, interviewing adult household members (>18 years) excluding health worker households.
Core methods
Data were collected using interviewer-administered questionnaires on socio-demographics, mpox knowledge, and perceived threat, with analysis using descriptive and inferential statistics (ANOVA, logistic regression) via SPSS v26.
Main results
Only 24.6% (192/782) had good mpox knowledge, with positive perceived threat at 45.1% and positive efficacy at 79.9% (625/782). Threat-efficacy interaction significantly predicted knowledge (p<0.001, ηp2=0.022), with factors like education level and occupation showing significant associations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret low mpox knowledge as indicating a need for robust public health interventions, with perceived threat and efficacy interacting to shape knowledge, a novel application of the Extended Parallel Process Model in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings suggest interventions should address knowledge gaps, pair perceived threat with efficacy, and leverage mass and social media, which are preferred information sources (62.4% and 23.3%, respectively).
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the abstract-only evidence restricts assessment of potential biases; however, the large sample size and systematic sampling provide moderate confidence in the findings.

艾滋病毒感染者的免疫(功能障碍)功能

Immune (dys)function in people living with HIV
作者： Eva Theresa Grüner
摘要中文翻译艾滋病毒感染者需要量身定制的医疗保健策略,包括病毒(合并)感染的管理。为了实现这一目标,全面了解针对 HIV 本身和 猴痘病毒 等病原体的免疫(功能障碍)功能至关重要。本文提出的两篇出版物研究了在 HIV 专业诊所和慕尼黑大学门诊接受医疗护理的 HIV 感染者的适应性 免疫应答,重点是 T 细胞免疫。患者 队列研究 由正在开始或已经接受抗逆转录病毒治疗的个体组成。接受治疗的患者表现出无法检测到的血浆病毒载量和稳定的 CD4 T 细胞计数——除了个别病例。我们对滤泡 (CXCR5+) CD8 T 细胞进行了深入表征:这是一种能够进入 B 细胞滤泡的亚群,而滤泡是潜伏 HIV 储存宿主 的一个主要位点。我们发现这些细胞的出现频率与完整的前病毒 HIV DNA 水平呈负相关,并且,特别是在急性感染期间接受治疗的个体中,滤泡 CD8 T 细胞表现出抗 HIV 功能,这种功能可以通过体外抑制 PD1 来进一步增强。由于其特性,滤泡 CD8 T 细胞可能在未来的功能性治疗策略中发挥核心作用,特别是对于在急性 HIV 感染期间早期开始抗逆转录病毒治疗的个体。正如最近在 2022 年 猴痘 暴发 期间所强调的那样,HIV 感染者强大的 抗病毒药物 免疫力的相关性超出了 HIV 本身。因此,我们研究了 HIV 感染者在 猴痘 感染或天花 疫苗接种 后的细胞和体液适应性 免疫应答。我们可以证明,感染和 猴痘 都会引发 猴痘 特异性 免疫应答,涉及 T 细胞和抗体反应,包括在具有稳定免疫功能的 HIV 感染者中。总的来说,这些研究强调需要针对该人群独特的免疫状况制定量身定制的预防、诊断和治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HIV 感染者面临着病毒合并感染(包括 猴痘)的独特风险,因此需要量身定制的医疗保健策略。该研究旨在阐明该人群的免疫(功能障碍)功能,特别是对 HIV 和 猴痘病毒 的反应,为有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究调查了在艾滋病毒专业诊所和慕尼黑大学门诊部接受护理的艾滋病毒感染者的适应性 免疫应答。患者 队列研究 包括开始或已经接受抗逆转录病毒治疗的个体。
核心方法
该研究表征了滤泡 (CXCR5+) CD8 T 细胞,这是一种能够进入 B 细胞滤泡的亚群,而 B 细胞滤泡是潜伏 HIV 储存宿主 的主要位点。作者评估了这些细胞的抗 HIV 功能,并评估了体外 PD1 抑制的效果。
主要结果
除个别病例外,接受抗逆转录病毒治疗的个体表现出不可检测的血浆病毒载量和稳定的 CD4 T 细胞计数。滤泡 CD8 T 细胞的频率与完整的前病毒 HIV DNA 水平呈负相关,这些细胞表现出抗 HIV 功能,特别是在急性感染期间接受治疗的细胞中。
解释与创新
作者提出,滤泡 CD8 T 细胞可能在未来的 HIV 功能性治疗策略中发挥核心作用,特别是在急性感染早期开始抗逆转录病毒治疗时。这代表了对具有潜在治疗意义的特定 T 细胞亚群的新关注。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要为艾滋病毒感染者制定量身定制的预防、诊断和治疗策略,以适应其独特的免疫状况,特别是在 猴痘 等合并感染的情况下。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计细节、样本量、统计分析或更广泛的研究结果的普遍性。证据仅限于抽象,缺乏方法论和具体结果的细节,需要谨慎解释。

Immune (dys)function in people living with HIV

Authors: Eva Theresa Grüner
Original abstract (English)
People living with HIV require tailored healthcare strategies, including the management of viral (co)infections. To achieve this, a comprehensive understanding of immune (dys)function in response to pathogens such as HIV itself and Mpox virus is essential. The two publications presented herein investigate the adaptive immune response, with a focus on T cell immunity, in people living with HIV receiving medical care at HIV-specialized practices and at a university outpatient clinic in Munich. The patient cohort consists of individuals either initiating or already undergoing antiretroviral therapy. Those under therapy showed undetectable plasma viral loads and stable CD4 T cell counts – except for isolated cases. We conducted an in-depth characterization of follicular (CXCR5+) CD8 T cells: a subpopulation capable of accessing B cell follicles, which is one major site of the latent HIV reservoir. We found that the frequency of these cells negatively correlates with the level of intact pro-viral HIV DNA and that, particularly in individuals treated during acute infections, follicular CD8 T cells exhibit anti-HIV functionality, which can be further enhanced by PD1 inhibition in-vitro. Due to their characteristics, follicular CD8 T cells may play a central role in future functional cure strategies, especially in individuals initiating antiretroviral therapy early during acute HIV infection. The relevance of robust antiviral immunity in people living with HIV extends beyond HIV itself, as recently underlined during the Mpox outbreak in 2022. Therefore, we investigated cellular and humoral adaptive immune responses after Mpox infection or smallpox vaccination in people living with HIV. We could show that both infection and vaccination elicited Mpox-specific immune responses involving both T cells and antibody responses including in people living with HIV with a stable immune function. Taken together, these studies emphasize the need for tailored preventive, diagnostic and therapeutic strategies that account for the unique immune landscape of this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
People living with HIV face distinct risks from viral co-infections, including mpox, necessitating tailored healthcare strategies. The study aimed to elucidate immune (dys)function in this population, particularly in response to HIV and mpox virus, to inform targeted interventions.
Design and population
The research investigated adaptive immune responses in people living with HIV receiving care at HIV-specialized practices and a university outpatient clinic in Munich. The patient cohort included individuals initiating or already undergoing antiretroviral therapy.
Core methods
The study characterized follicular (CXCR5+) CD8 T cells, a subpopulation capable of accessing B cell follicles, which are major sites of the latent HIV reservoir. The authors assessed anti-HIV functionality of these cells and evaluated the effect of PD1 inhibition in vitro.
Main results
Individuals under antiretroviral therapy exhibited undetectable plasma viral loads and stable CD4 T cell counts, except in isolated cases. The frequency of follicular CD8 T cells negatively correlated with the level of intact pro-viral HIV DNA, and these cells showed anti-HIV functionality, particularly in those treated during acute infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors propose that follicular CD8 T cells may play a central role in future functional cure strategies for HIV, especially when antiretroviral therapy is initiated early during acute infection. This represents a novel focus on a specific T cell subpopulation with potential therapeutic implications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for tailored preventive, diagnostic, and therapeutic strategies for people living with HIV that account for their unique immune landscape, particularly in the context of co-infections like mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report the study design details, sample size, statistical analyses, or broader generalizability of the findings. The evidence is limited to the abstract and lacks methodological and outcome-specific details, warranting a cautious interpretation.

埃博拉病毒的持久性和免疫特权的隐性成本

Ebola virus persistence and the hidden cost of immune privilege
作者： Amal Fahmi, Isabel Schultz-Pernice, Marco P. Alves, Fahmi A, Schultz-Pernice I, Alves MP.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解埃博拉病毒如何在中枢神经系统的免疫特权环境中建立持续感染,解决有关人类神经组织慢性感染和复发机制的知识差距。
设计与对象
研究人员开发了一种人脑类器官模型来分析人类神经组织中的正埃博拉病毒感染,在长达 120 天的时间内测试了埃博拉病毒、苏丹病毒、雷斯顿病毒和马尔堡病毒。
核心方法
该研究使用人脑类器官来研究病毒的持久性,检查病毒通过出芽和直接细胞间 传播 的传播,以及病毒 进化 和有缺陷的病毒 基因组 的积累。
主要结果
EBOV 在多种神经细胞类型中持续存在长达 120 天,包括神经元、星形胶质细胞、小胶质细胞和少突胶质细胞,其持续存在与病毒 进化 的持续存在和缺陷病毒 基因组 的积累有关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,埃博拉病毒利用中枢神经系统的免疫特权状态,通过细胞间传播和有缺陷的病毒 基因组 等机制建立长期持久性,为病毒适应神经环境提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调中枢神经系统是埃博拉病毒持续存在的潜在储存宿主,这可能导致幸存者复发和慢性神经炎症,从而为监测和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于对类器官模型的依赖,该模型可能无法完全复制人类中枢神经系统的复杂性,并且其他正埃博拉病毒物种的机制保守性存在不确定性,因此证据强度中等。

Ebola virus persistence and the hidden cost of immune privilege

Authors: Amal Fahmi, Isabel Schultz-Pernice, Marco P. Alves, Fahmi A, Schultz-Pernice I, Alves MP.
Original abstract (English)
As a result, immune-privileged niches may be co-opted by pathogens as long-term reservoirs and enable persistence despite systemic immune surveillance. Mechanistically, the study shows that EBOV persistence relies on both canonical viral budding and direct cell-to-cell transmission through actin-dependent cellular projections and syncytia formation. Importantly, the study iden- tifies marked cell type-specific differences in the host response to persistent EBOV infection. More broadly, persistent viral reservoirs represent one of sev- eral proposed mechanisms contributing to post-acute sequelae of infection, including long COVID, in which sustained immune activation may result from a combination of viral persistence, immune dysregula- tion and other host factors8,9. nature microbiology https://doi.org/10.1038/s41564-026-02432-1 News & views Viral infection Ebola virus persistence and the hidden cost of immune privilege Amal Fahmi, Isabel Schultz-Pernice & Marco P. Alves Human brain organoids reveal how Ebola virus establishes persistent infection within the immune-privileged environment of the central nervous system. The idea of immune privilege has reshaped our understanding of how the immune system is regulated across tissues. It is viewed as a state of selective immune tolerance characterized by tissue- and organ-specific immune regulation. Classic examples include the central nervous sys- tem (CNS), eyes and testes, where immune surveillance is present but constrained. These sites maintain a highly regulated immunological milieu through cellular barriers, local immunoregulatory mediators and context-dependent modulation of antigen presentation. While this restrained inflammatory tone is essential to preserve the function and integrity of indispensable tissues, it c...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how Ebola virus establishes persistent infection within the immune-privileged environment of the central nervous system, addressing the knowledge gap regarding the mechanisms underlying chronic infection and relapse in human neural tissue.
Design and population
The researchers developed a human brain organoid model to analyze orthoebolavirus infection in human neural tissue, testing Ebola virus, Sudan virus, Reston virus, and Marburg virus over a period of up to 120 days.
Core methods
The study used human brain organoids to investigate viral persistence, examining viral spread through budding and direct cell-to-cell transmission, as well as viral evolution and accumulation of defective viral genomes.
Main results
EBOV persisted for up to 120 days in diverse neural cell types, including neurons, astrocytes, microglia, and oligodendrocytes, with persistence associated with ongoing viral evolution and accumulation of defective viral genomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that EBOV exploits the CNS's immune-privileged status to establish long-term persistence through mechanisms like cell-to-cell spread and defective viral genomes, providing novel insights into viral adaptation to neural environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance and prevention strategies by highlighting the CNS as a potential reservoir for EBOV persistence, which could lead to relapses and chronic neuroinflammation in survivors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on an organoid model, which may not fully replicate human CNS complexity, and the uncertainty regarding conservation of mechanisms across other Orthoebolavirus species.
